HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000473/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_1959dccd3215b9cb0e3b","text":"1. **The question causes knowledge by controlled delay.** `د ر ي B001` includes knowing and causing another to know. The pronoun `هِيَهْ` points back to the feminine predicate in 101:9 without naming its substance; 101:11 then supplies the terse answer. Withholding followed by identification gives `أَدْرَىٰكَ` the role of staging disclosure. The packet provides anaphora and answer, while the claim that delay is the means of instruction is abductive. The ayah reads as an active knowledge-producing interval rather than an empty rhetorical pause.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000473/B001","root_000473/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_31126c866fa2313efce6","text":"The sole later ayah, 101:11, completed the causative mechanism of `د ر ي B001`: the unresolved pronoun receives a compact nominal answer. Because `ن و ر B001` also carries illumination, the answer does not merely add information; it retrospectively casts the question as a controlled movement from opacity to disclosure. No later evidence strengthened the hunting-concealment branch `د ر ي B003`.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000473/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_3cf7905801e6e8e8655d","text":"3. **Exploratory semantic teasing after material teasing.** `د ر ي B004` supplies a sharp horn or comb-like implement. At the far edge of this focus window, 101:5 has `ٱلْعِهْنِ ٱلْمَنفُوشِ`; `ن ف ش B001` explicitly involves teasing or beating wool until fibers separate. The unresolved `هِيَهْ` is then semantically separated into the answer `نَارٌ حَامِيَةٌ`. The packet supplies the comb-like branch, fluffed wool, and question-answer sequence; analogizing lexical clarification to combing is abductive and exploratory. The question thereby reads as an instrument that teases apart a compressed predicate until its internal referent becomes visible.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000473/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_429610c2168fdaeea8b1","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:10:2\",\"root_000473\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Gizli avı izleme çağrışımı, açıklamayı ateşle görünür olacak saklı bir nesneye kontrollü yaklaşım gibi kurar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000473/B001","root_000473/B002","root_000473/B003","root_000473/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_4a086d515dc005a6b68a","text":"1. Second epistemic blow, now reduced to \"it.\" Evidence: د ر ي B001, knowing/causing to know; ق ر ع B006 from 101:1-3, striking warning; ه و ي B002 in 101:9, Hawiyah as abyss. Structural cue: the formula of 101:3 returns, but القارعة is replaced by the pronoun هيه. Mechanism: the first unknown event narrows into a second unknown referent whose identity is still withheld. Abductive move: I infer intensification by pronominal compression. Reading change: the ayah makes Hawiyah more uncanny: nearer in grammar, still beyond grasp. 2. Directed approach to the hidden target. Evidence: د ر ي B002, aiming/setting upon; د ر ي B003, stalking concealed game; ن و ر B005 in 101:11, beacon/visible marker. Structural cue: \"what makes you know\" immediately precedes the final disclosure, نار حامية. Mechanism: the question aims the hearer at the concealed object, and the next ayah flashes it into view. Abductive move: I supply the hunting/targeting model for the delayed reveal. Reading change: أدراك becomes guided approach to the fire, not merely intellectual notification. 3. Cutting away false speech. Evidence: د ر ي B004, sharp horn/comb implement; ه و ي B010, false and idle speech; ع ه ن B006 in 101:5, speech cast without reflection. Structural cue: the surah's repeated questions forbid easy verbal filling. Mechanism: the sharp knowing-instrument strips away careless speech until only the final terse answer remains. Abductive move: I infer a corrective function from the contrast between speculative speech branches and the compressed answer. Reading change: the ayah reads as discipline of language before eschatic reality.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000473/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_4a6c93fc32f55fd73c8a","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:10:2\",\"root_000473\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Soru, zamiri bir an açıklamadan tutup ardından ateşle karşılayarak gecikmeyi bilgi üretme aracına dönüştürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000473/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_82c74e01d45f7268bcf5","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:10:2\",\"root_000473\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Yönelme boyutu, dikkati gerideki uçurumdan ilerideki açıklamaya bağlanan tek bir hedefe odaklar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000473/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_97a2144a8f939de89433","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:10:2\",\"root_000473\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Sivri ya da tarak benzeri araç çağrışımı, sıkıştırılmış zamirin anlamını son cevaba doğru ayırıp açan bir işlem düşündürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000473/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_b910a4e73eefd65101dd","text":"2. **Attention is aimed from pronoun to referent.** Exploratorily, `د ر ي B002` supplies aiming at and setting upon something. The repeated `مَا` and the minimal `هِيَهْ` narrow attention onto one unresolved referent between `هَاوِيَةٌ` and `نَارٌ حَامِيَةٌ`. This branch assigns the question a deictic role, directing cognition backward to the abyss and forward to its specification. Mapping raid-oriented aiming onto reference resolution is the abductive and weaker step. It makes the line a precise act of attentional targeting, not a general profession of ignorance.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000473/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_c89d37bc0c36a1f7ddbb","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:10:2\",\"root_000473\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Keskin araç çağrışımı, olası boş sözleri budayıp yalnız kısa maddi cevabı bırakıyormuş gibi okunabilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_e8ef8c78692a34e795dd","text":"101:11 immediately answered the pronoun with fire, confirming that the second أدراك formula delays disclosure only for one beat.
